<script>
  document.addEventListener('grecaptchaExecute', function(event) {
    console.log('executing recaptcha')
    grecaptcha.execute(event.detail.recaptchaSiteKey, {action: event.detail.action}).then(function(token) {
      console.log('dispatching event back')
      document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('grecaptchaVerified', {'detail': {
        response: token, 
        action: event.detail.action, 
        recaptchaSiteKey: event.detail.recaptchaSiteKey
      }}));
    });
  }); 
  document.addEventListener('grecaptchaReset', function() {
        try {
            console.log('reseting token');
            grecaptcha.reset();
        } catch(err){}
  }); 
</script>

<style id="themeCSS"></style><style id="customCSS">[data-type="MilestoneEvent"]{display:none !important;}
.communityH2Style{
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    color: #666666 !important;
    line-height: 30px;
    font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
}

.forceCommunityThemeNav .mainNavItem:first-child {
    visibility: hidden;
    display: none;
}

@font-face {
    font-family: 'Gibson-Regular-webfont';
    src: url('/Community2/s/sfsites/c/resource/CommunityFonts/gibson-woff/Gibson-Regular-webfont.woff') format('woff');

}

.grecaptcha-badge{
    z-index:1;
}

.forceCommunityRecordHomeTabs .cuf-media-right {
    display:none !important;
}
.forceCommunityRecordHomeTabs .slds-post__footer {
    display:none !important;
}
.forceCommunityRecordHomeTabs .slds-feed__item-comments {
    display:none !important;
}

.article-head selfServiceArticleHeaderDetail h1{
          font-size: 12px;
    color: #666666 !important; 
    line-height: 18px;
    font-weight:400px;
}

.selfServiceArticleHeaderDetail {
     font-size: 16px;
    color: #666666 !important; 
    line-height: 18px;
    font-weight:600px;
}

.summary h1 {
       font-size: 2.25rem;
    color: #2F6DB5; 
}
.slds-global-header__item--search { background-color: white; }

.slds-global-header__item--search .slds-input { color:white; }

.topicHierarchy-topBox .topicHierarchy-top span {
       font-size: 2.25rem;
    color: #2F6DB5; 
    line-height: 44px;
   padding-bottom:20px;
     padding-top:20px;

}

.topicHierarchy-topBox .topicHierarchy-childrenList .topicHierarchy-child span {
          font-size: 1.5rem;
    color: #666666; 
    line-height: 30px;
   

}

.topicHierarchy-topBox .topicHierarchy-childrenList span {
          font-size: 1.5rem;
    color: #666666; 
    line-height: 30px;
   

}

.comm-related-articles .comm-related-articles__item span
{
      font-size: 16px;
    font-weight:400;
	font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
}

.comm-related-articles .comm-related-articles__item span
{
      font-size: 16px;
	  font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
    font-weight:400;
}
.comm-topic-trending-articles .comm-topic-trending-articles__item span
{
	font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
      font-size: 16px;
    font-weight:400;
}
.comm-related-articles > h2{
      padding-top:40px;
      background-color: #f4f4f4;
      color: #2F6DB5;
     font-size: 2.25rem;
    line-height: 44px;
    font-weight:600;
    font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
    text-align: center;
}
.comm-related-articles ul
{
    text-decoration:none !important;
    background-color: #f4f4f4;
   padding:40px;
    list-style-type: none;
     border:none;
}
.comm-related-articles__item a {
    text-decoration:none !important;
    border-style:none;
     background-color: #f4f4f4;
    font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
}

.comm-related-articles__item div {
    text-decoration:none !important;
    border-style:none;
     background-color: #f4f4f4;
}
.comm-topic-trending-articles__item a {
    text-decoration:none !important;
    border-style:none;
    font-size: 35px !important;
    font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
}


.profile.uiMenuItem{
    display: none;
}
.selfServiceUserProfileMenu
{
    display: none;
    color: #ffffff;

}
.myAccount{
    display: none; 
    }

.home{
    display: none; 
    }

h2 {
    font-size: 2.25rem;
    color: #2F6DB5; 
    line-height: 44px;
    font-family: sans-serif;
}

h3 {
    font-size: 1.5rem;
    color: #666666; 
    line-height: 30px;
          font-family: sans-serif;
}



.comm-topic-trending-articles ul{
    text-decoration:none !important;
    background-color: #f4f4f4;
   	padding:40px;
    list-style-type: none;
     border:none;
}


.comm-topic-trending-articles h2{
	font-family: Gibson-Regular-webfont;
    background-color: #f4f4f4;
    text-align:center;
    color:#666666;
    font-weight:600;
    padding-top:20px;
}

.forceCommunityRichText ul>li:before{
    content:"" !important;
}



ul.no-bullets {
  list-style-type: none; /* Remove bullets */
  padding: 0; /* Remove padding */
  margin: 0; /* Remove margins */
    
}
.topicItem {
    padding : 10px !important;
}

.topicImage{
    background-size: contain !important;
}
.forceTopicFeaturedTopics .topicLabel{
    color:#2F6DB5 !important;
    font-size:15px !important;
}
.slds-card__header{
    display: none;
}

.forceTopicFeaturedTopics .topicItem a{
    border-radius: 5%;
    border-style: solid;
}
.forceTopicFeaturedTopics .topicContent {
    background-color: white;
}
.forceTopicFeaturedTopics .topicLabel{
    font-size:15px !important;
}
.forceCommunityThemeHeroStarter .searchRegion{
    display:none;

function initFramework () {
	window.Aura = window.Aura || {};
	window.Aura.app = auraConfig["context"]["app"];
	window.Aura.beforeFrameworkInit = Aura.beforeFrameworkInit || [];
	window.Aura.beforeFrameworkInit.push(auraPreInitBlock);
	window.Aura.inlineJsReady = time();

	;

	if (!window.Aura.frameworkJsReady) {
		window.Aura.initConfig = auraConfig;
	} else {
		if (auraConfig.context) {
			if (auraConfig.context.dpbp) {
				// Set a data attribute on document.body to signal engine to bypass global patching
				document.body.setAttribute("data-global-patching-bypass", "temporary-bypass");
			}
		}

		// LockerService must be initialized before scripts can be executed.
		$A.lockerService.initialize(auraConfig.context);

		// scripts inside custom templates with Locker active are stored
		// until now since we need LockerService before running

		var scripts = window.Aura.inlineJsLocker;
		if (scripts) {
			for (var i = 0; i < scripts.length; i++) {
				$A.lockerService.runScript(scripts[i]["callback"], scripts[i]["namespace"]);
			}

			delete window.Aura.inlineJsLocker;
		}

		if (true) {
			$A.initAsync(auraConfig);
		} else if (false) {

		}
	}

    });
}
// Error msg
var x = document.getElementById("dismissError");
var errorReload = document.getElementById("auraErrorReload");
var auraErrorMask = document.getElementById("auraErrorMask");
var err = document.getElementById("auraError");
function closeErrorBox() {
	if (window["$A"]) {
		$A.util.removeClass(auraErrorMask, "auraForcedErrorBox");
		$A.util.removeClass($A.util.getElement("auraErrorReload"), "show");
	} else {
		document.body.removeChild(auraErrorMask);
	}
}
function squash(e) {
	e.preventDefault();
	e.stopPropagation();
}
if (x) {
	x.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
		closeErrorBox();
		squash(e);
	});
}

if (err) {
	err.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
		if (e.key === 'Escape') {
			squash(e);
			closeErrorBox();
		} else if (e.key === 'Tab') {
			// Focus trap
			squash(e);
			if (e.target === x && errorReload && errorReload.classList.contains('show')) {
				errorReload.focus();
			} else if (e.target === errorReload) {
				x.focus();
			}
		}
	});
}

if (auraErrorMask) {
	var errorMaskObserver = new MutationObserver(function(ms) {
		ms.forEach(function(mut) {
			if (mut.type === 'attributes' && mut.attributeName === 'class' && x && auraErrorMask.classList.contains('auraForcedErrorBox')) {
				requestAnimationFrame(function() { x.focus(); });
			}
		});
	});

	errorMaskObserver.observe(auraErrorMask, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
}

setTimeout(initFramework, 0); // ensure async



var appCssLoadingCount = 0;
function onLoadStyleSheets(event) {
	if(event){
		var element = event.target;
		element.removeEventListener("load",onLoadStyleSheets);
		element.removeEventListener("error",onLoadStyleSheetsError);
	}
	window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoading = (--appCssLoadingCount) > 0;
	if (!window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoading) {
		if (typeof window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoadedCallback === "function") {
			window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoadedCallback();
			window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoadedCallback = undefined;
		}
	}
}
function onLoadStyleSheetsError(event) {
	window.Aura.bootstrap.hasCssLoadError = true;
	onLoadStyleSheets(event);
}

var auraCss = document.getElementsByClassName("auraCss"),
	current;
window.Aura.bootstrap.appCssLoading = auraCss.length > 0;
for (var c=0,length=auraCss.length;c<length;c++) {
	current = auraCss[c];
	appCssLoadingCount++;
	current.addEventListener("load",onLoadStyleSheets);
	current.addEventListener("error",onLoadStyleSheetsError);
	current.href = current.getAttribute("data-href");
}

}
